SRI LANKA INSTITUTE OF ARCHITECTS

New Registration and Renewal of Registration of “Chartered Architects”, “Architects” and “Architectural Licentiates”for the Year 2015/2016 under the Architects Registration Board, established as per the Sri Lanka Institute ofArchitects (Amendment) Act, No 14 of 1996

REFERENCE is made to the notes appearing on pages 1A, 2A, 3A, 4A and 5A of the Gazette of the Democratic SocialistRepublic of Sri Lanka (Extraordinary) No. 1015 / 19 of Friday, the 20th February, 1998 with regard to the Registration ofpersons who are eligible to practice Architecture in Sri Lanka under the Categories “Chartered Architects”, “Architects” andArchitectural “Licentiates”. The interpretations of the Sri Lanka Institute of Architects (Amendment) Act, No. 14 of 1996 in theabove Gazette Notification will apply in all respects to the list published below.

The Architects Registration Board of the Sri Lanka Institute of Architects as per the functions and powers vestedupon it by the said Act, of Parliament has now prepared the list of the “Chartered Architects”, “Architects” and “ArchitecturalLicentiates” who are eligible to be Gazetted for the Year 2015/2016 (01st February 2015 to 31st January 2016).

The Architects Registration Board directed me to publish the Names, Addresses and Registration Numbers of the“Chartered Architects”, “Architects” and “Architectural Licentiates” who have been registered as given below.

This registration expires on 31st January, 2016.

E. G. G. M. ELLEPOLAGEDARA,Registrar,Architects Registration Board.

Sri Lanka Institute of Architects,No. 120/7, Vidya Mawatha, Colombo 07,26th January 2015.
